| OBJECT-SAFETY-AUDIT-GATE | A validator confirming every trait with `async fn` used behind `dyn`/`Arc<dyn>` in any spec carries `#[async_trait]`, and every `impl` of such a trait carries `#[async_trait]`. Root cause: object-safety defects surfaced one-per-round (DynRunnable R33, VectorStoreRetriever-adjacent R32, VP-016 R31) until a comprehensive corpus-wide audit in R33 found 5 latent E0038 defects at once (CheckpointSaver/GuardrailHook/MemoryStore traits + 2 ADR-029 impls) and fixed them in-scope — preempting 3-5 future rounds. Without a gate, the class risk recurs whenever new traits or impls are added. EXTENDED (round-34/F-P2A144-01): layer-2 check — native-async supertraits bridged to `#[async_trait]` Send-boxed façades (or stored behind `Arc<dyn>`) must carry `+ Send` RPITIT on their async methods so the boxed future is provably Send on stable Rust (E0277). Fixing layer 1 (façade carries `#[async_trait]`) exposes layer 2 (supertrait RPITIT future not provably Send; blanket impl fails).
